=== SHEET: Overview ===
Rehabilitation Services Administration
Program Year 2024 - WIOA Performance in Review
WIOA State Performance and Supporting Historical Data
Overview:
RSA is providing this excel workbook as a summary of actual performance in program year 2024. It provides state rankings by performance measure for each indicator with 4 additional years of historical data. Also included are indicator performance rankings for blind and general programs, state level cohort data for participants, exiters, and the characteristics of participants found in the numerator of each performance measure. National averages may also be found for a range of operational statistics. 
Purpose:
RSA understands that contextual information is an important consideration for state agencies as they pursue optimal program performance . The data provided in this workbook will assist VR agencies gain a broader perspective by viewing their own performance in the context of their peers in other states and at the national level.
Important Considerations:
This workbook does not contain assessment results for program year 2024. Final assessment of state indicator scores and program scores will be released in coordination with the Department of Labor's Employment and Training Administration in mid to late February of 2026.  
WIOA performance data in this workbook are derived from the ETA-9169 and matches what has been historically reported and published.
Please contact your RSA Data Unit representative or your State Liaison to discuss any questions you may have.  A link to contact information provided below.
Navigation and Selection of Content:
Performance Measure Comparisons by State
Performance Measure Comparisons for Blind Agencies
Performance Measure Comparisons for General Agencies

End of Sheet
